defaults\n            data = {\n                \"page_number\": i + 1,\n                \"packet_ref\": None,\n                \"revision_no\": None,\n                \"participant_name\": None,\n                \"payment_token\": None,\n                \"award_ref\": None,\n                \"requested_amount\": None,\n            }\n            for line in lines[1:]:\n                if line.startswith('Packet Ref:'):\n                    data['packet_ref'] = line.split(':', 1)[1].strip()\n                elif line.startswith('Revision No:'):\n                    try:\n                        data['revision_no'] = int(line.split(':', 1)[1].strip())\n                    except ValueError:\n                        data['revision_no'] = None\n                elif line.startswith('Participant'):\n                    # find max revision\n        max_rev = max(item['revision_no'] for item in items)\n        candidates = [it for it in items if it['revision_no'] == max_rev]\n        # if tie, keep highest page_number\n        chosen = max(candidates, key=lambda x: x['page_number'])\n        retained.append(chosen)\n    return retained\n\ndef load_registry():\n    xl = pd.ExcelFile(REGISTRY_XLSX)\n    participants_df = xl.parse('participants')\n    aliases_df = xl.parse('aliases')\n    # alias mapping: code -> list of alias names\n    alias_map = {}\n    for _, row in aliases_df.iterrows():\n        code = row['participant_code']\n        alias = row['alias_name']\n        alias_map.setdefault(code, []).append(alias)\n    # Build lookup structures\n    # normalized name -> code (if collisions, keep first)\n    norm_to_code = {}\n    for code, name in name_by_code.items():\n        norm = normalize(name)\n        norm_to_code[norm] = code\n        for alias in alias_map.get(code, []):\n            norm_to_code[normalize(alias)] = code\n    # also keep full list for fuzzy matching\n    all_names = []  # list of (code, original_name)\n    for code, name in name_by_code.items():\n        all_names.append((code, name))\n        for alias in alias_map.get(code, []):\n            all_names.append((code, alias))\n    return token_by_code, name_by_code, alias_map, norm_to_code, all_names\n\ndef load_awards():\n    with open(AWARD_JSON) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    awards = {}\n    for sponsor in data.get('sponsors', []):\n        for program in sponsor.get('programs', []):\n            for aw in program.get('awards', []):\n                if aw.get('status') != 'active':\n                    continue\n                ref = aw.get('award_ref')\n                awards[ref] = {\n                    'approved_amount': float(aw.get('approved_amount')),\n                    'participant_code': aw.get('participant_code')\n                }\n    return awards\n\ndef load_version_overrides():\n    df = pd.read_csv(VERSIONS_CSV, dtype=str)\n    # Filter rows\n    df = df[(df['approval_state'] == 'approved') &\n            (df['version_amount'].notna()) & (df['version_amount'] != '') &\n            (df['participant_code'].notna()) & (df['participant_code'] != '')]\n    df['version_no'] = pd.to_numeric(df['version_no'], errors='coerce')\n    df['version_amount'] = pd.to_numeric(df['version_amount'], errors='coerce')\n    overrides = {}\n    for ref, group in df.groupby('award_ref'):\n        # keep highest version_no\n        best = group.loc[group['version_no'].idxmax()]\n        overrides[ref] = {\n            'approved_amount': float(best['version_amount']),\n            'participant_code': best['participant_code']\n        }\n    return overrides\n\ndef apply_overrides(awards, overrides):\n    for ref, ov in overrides.items():\n        if ref in awards:\n            awards[ref] = ov\n    return awards\n\ndef match_participant(name, all_names, norm_to_code):\n    norm = normalize(name)\n    # exact normalized match first\n    if norm in norm_to_code:\n        return norm_to_code[norm]\n    # fuzzy match using rapidfuzz\n    best_score = 0\n    best_code = None\n    for code, cand in all_names:\n        score = fuzz.ratio(normalize(cand), norm)\n        if score > best_score:\n            best_score = score\n            best_code = code\n    if best_score >= 85:\n        return best_code\n    return None\n\ndef main():\n    pages = parse_pdf()\n    retained = deduplicate(pages)\n    token_by_code, name_by_code, alias_map, norm_to_code, all_names = load_registry()\n    awards = load_awards()\n    overrides = load_version_overrides()\n    awards = apply_overrides(awards, overrides)\n    flagged = []\n    for p in retained:\n        reason = None\n        # 1. Unknown Participant\n        matched_code = match_participant(p['participant_name'], all_names, norm_to_code)\n        if not matched_code:\n            reason = 'Unknown Participant'\n        else:\n            # 2. Account Mismatch\n            expected_token = token_by_code.get(matched_code)\n            if expected_token != p['payment_token']:\n                reason = 'Account Mismatch'\n            else:\n                # 3. Invalid Award Ref\n                award_ref = p['award_ref']\n                if not award_ref or award_ref not in awards:\n                    reason = 'Invalid Award Ref'\n                else:\n                    award = awards[award_ref]\n                    # 4. Amount Mismatch\n                    if abs(p['requested_amount'] - award['approved_amount']) > 0.01:\n                        reason = 'Amount Mismatch'\n                    else:\n                        # 5. Participant Mismatch\n                        if award['participant_code'] != matched_code:\n                            reason = 'Participant Mismatch'\n        if reason:\n            flagged.append({\n                'request_page_number': p['page_number'],\n                'participant_name': p['participant_name'],\n                'requested_amount': p['requested_amount'],\n                'payment_token': p['payment_token'],\n                'award_ref': p['award_ref'],\n                'reason': reason\n            })\n    # sort\n    flagged.sort(key=lambda x: x['request_page_number'])\n    # write output\n    with open(OUTPUT_JSON, 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(flagged, f, indent=2)\n    # validation output\n    with open(OUTPUT_JSON) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    print(json.dumps(data, indent=2))\n    print(f\"Total flagged entries: {len(data)}\")\n    if data:\n        pages_nums = [entry['request_page_number'] for entry in data]\n        print('Flagged page numbers:', pages_nums)\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"}}],"model":"openai/gpt-oss-120b:free","stop_reason":null,"stop_details":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":null,"cache_read_input_tokens":null,"cache_creation":null,"inference_geo":null,"server_tool_use":null,"service_tier":null,"speed":"standard"},"provider":"OpenInference","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"7eee9d49-b37e-4214-8b9c-1aff15c11e4d","uuid":"7f25e71e-91ff-4e6e-bee3-c36c6f21109f"}
